#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

using Real = double;

// 3D index calculation
inline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {
    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;
}

void initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                grid[idx] = (idx % 19) * 1.0;
            }
        }
    }
}

// 7-point stencil computation
void stencilIteration(const std::vector<Real>& input, 
                      std::vector<Real>& output,
                      const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
    // Process interior points (not on boundaries)
    for (size_t z = 1; z < nz - 1; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 1; y < ny - 1; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                
                const Real center = input[idx];
                const Real left = input[idx3(x-1, y, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real right = input[idx3(x+1, y, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real front = input[idx3(x, y-1, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real back = input[idx3(x, y+1, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real bottom = input[idx3(x, y, z-1, nx, ny)];
                const Real top = input[idx3(x, y, z+1, nx, ny)];
                
                // Simple averaging stencil
                output[idx] = (center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top) / 7.0;
            }
        }
    }
    
    // Copy boundary values
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                if (x == 0 || x == nx-1 || y == 0 || y == ny-1 || z == 0 || z == nz-1) {
                    const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                    output[idx] = input[idx];
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<Real>& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {
    // Simple sanity checks
    
    // 1. No NaN or Inf values
    for (const auto& val : grid) {
        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)
    Real minVal = grid[0];
    Real maxVal = grid[0];
    for (const auto& val : grid) {
        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);
        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);
    }
    
    printf("Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", minVal, maxVal);
    
    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded
    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {
        printf("Validation failed: values out of expected range\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly
    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\n");
    printf("  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t nx = 128;
    size_t ny = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified
    size_t nz = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified
    int iterations = 10;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-x") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-y") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-z") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;
    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;
    
    printf("3D Stencil Benchmark\n");
    printf("Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\n", nx, ny, nz);
    printf("Iterations: %d\n", iterations);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;
    
    // Allocate grids (double buffering)
    std::vector<Real> grid1(gridSize);
    std::vector<Real> grid2(gridSize);
    
    // Initialize
    printf("Initializing grid...\n");
    initializeGrid(grid1, nx, ny, nz);
    
    // Run stencil iterations
    printf("Running stencil computation...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
        if (iter % 2 == 0) {
            stencilIteration(grid1, grid2, nx, ny, nz);
        } else {
            stencilIteration(grid2, grid1, nx, ny, nz);
        }
    }
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate performance metrics
    double cellUpdates = (double)((nx-2) * (ny-2) * (nz-2)) * iterations;
    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;  // Million cell updates per second
    printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", mcups);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    const std::vector<Real>& finalGrid = (iterations % 2 == 0) ? grid1 : grid2;
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(finalGrid, "Grid");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(finalGrid, nx, ny, nz);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};
#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

using Real = double;

// 3D index calculation
inline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {
    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;
}

void initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                grid[idx] = (idx % 19) * 1.0;
            }
        }
    }
}

// 7-point stencil computation
void stencilIteration(const std::vector<Real>& input, 
                      std::vector<Real>& output,
                      const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
    // Process interior points (not on boundaries)
    for (size_t z = 1; z < nz - 1; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 1; y < ny - 1; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                
                const Real center = input[idx];
                const Real left = input[idx3(x-1, y, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real right = input[idx3(x+1, y, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real front = input[idx3(x, y-1, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real back = input[idx3(x, y+1, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real bottom = input[idx3(x, y, z-1, nx, ny)];
                const Real top = input[idx3(x, y, z+1, nx, ny)];
                
                // Simple averaging stencil
                output[idx] = (center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top) / 7.0;
            }
        }
    }
    
    // Copy boundary values
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                if (x == 0 || x == nx-1 || y == 0 || y == ny-1 || z == 0 || z == nz-1) {
                    const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                    output[idx] = input[idx];
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<Real>& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {
    // Simple sanity checks
    
    // 1. No NaN or Inf values
    for (const auto& val : grid) {
        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)
    Real minVal = grid[0];
    Real maxVal = grid[0];
    for (const auto& val : grid) {
        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);
        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);
    }
    
    printf("Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", minVal, maxVal);
    
    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded
    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {
        printf("Validation failed: values out of expected range\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly
    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\n");
    printf("  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t nx = 128;
    size_t ny = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified
    size_t nz = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified
    int iterations = 10;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-x") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-y") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-z") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;
    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;
    
    printf("3D Stencil Benchmark\n");
    printf("Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\n", nx, ny, nz);
    printf("Iterations: %d\n", iterations);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;
    
    // Allocate grids (double buffering)
    std::vector<Real> grid1(gridSize);
    std::vector<Real> grid2(gridSize);
    
    // Initialize
    printf("Initializing grid...\n");
    initializeGrid(grid1, nx, ny, nz);
    
    // Run stencil iterations
    printf("Running stencil computation...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
        if (iter % 2 == 0) {
            stencilIteration(grid1, grid2, nx, ny, nz);
        } else {
            stencilIteration(grid2, grid1, nx, ny, nz);
        }
    }
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate performance metrics
    double cellUpdates = (double)((nx-2) * (ny-2) * (nz-2)) * iterations;
    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;  // Million cell updates per second
    printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", mcups);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    const std::vector<Real>& finalGrid = (iterations % 2 == 0) ? grid1 : grid2;
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(finalGrid, "Grid");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(finalGrid, nx, ny, nz);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};
#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

The design uses GPU-resident buffers and separate CUDA streams for slab interiors, slab edges, and halo transfers. MPI implementations with a CUDA-support query will use device halo buffers; others will use pinned host buffers. I’m preserving the original floating-point operation order so the result hashes can be compared exactly.

#include <algorithm>
#include <cerrno>
#include <climits>

#if __has_include(<mpi-ext.h>)
#include <mpi-ext.h>

#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"

using Real = double;

// Only the main thread calls MPI. Abort all ranks on errors, including those
// with no assigned planes, so peers cannot get stuck in a halo exchange.
static void mpiCheck(int status, const char* operation) {
    if (status == MPI_SUCCESS) return;
    char message[MPI_MAX_ERROR_STRING];
    int length = 0;
    MPI_Error_string(status, message, &length);
    fprintf(stderr, \"%s: %.*s\\n\", operation, length, message);
    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, status);
    std::abort();
}

static void cudaCheck(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
    if (status == cudaSuccess) return;
    int rank = 0;
    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
    fprintf(stderr, \"Rank %d, %s: %s\\n\", rank, operation,
            cudaGetErrorString(status));
    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
    std::abort();
}

#define MPI_CHECK(call) mpiCheck((call), #call)
#define CUDA_CHECK(call) cudaCheck((call), #call)

constexpr unsigned TileX = 32;
constexpr unsigned TileY = 8;
constexpr size_t TileZ = 8;

// Coalesced XY tiles share their four lateral neighbors. A register window
// reuses the center and the two Z neighbors while sweeping eight planes.
// Boundary mode updates just the two MPI faces, independently of bulk work.
template <bool Boundary>
__global__ void stencilKernel(const Real* __restrict__ input,
                              Real* __restrict__ output,
                              size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t zBegin,
                              size_t zEnd, size_t ownedPlanes,
                              size_t tilesX, size_t tilesY, size_t tiles) {
    __shared__ Real tile[TileY + 2][TileX + 2];
    const unsigned tx = threadIdx.x;
    const unsigned ty = threadIdx.y;
    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
    for (size_t block = blockIdx.x; block < tiles; block += gridDim.x) {
        const size_t bx = block % tilesX;
        const size_t byz = block / tilesX;
        const size_t by = byz % tilesY;
        const size_t bz = byz / tilesY;
        const size_t x = bx * TileX + tx + 1;
        const size_t y = by * TileY + ty + 1;
        const size_t first = Boundary ? (bz == 0 ? 1 : ownedPlanes)
                                     : zBegin + bz * TileZ;
        const size_t last = Boundary ? first + 1
                                    : (first + TileZ < zEnd ? first + TileZ : zEnd);
        // This predicate is uniform across the block.
        if (first < zBegin || first >= zEnd) continue;
        const bool load = x < nx && y < ny;
        const bool update = x < nx - 1 && y < ny - 1;
        size_t index = first * plane + y * nx + x;
        Real below = load ? input[index - plane] : 0.0;
        Real center = load ? input[index] : 0.0;
        for (size_t z = first; z < last; ++z, index += plane) {
            const Real above = load ? input[index + plane] : 0.0;
            tile[ty + 1][tx + 1] = center;
            if (tx == 0)
                tile[ty + 1][0] = load ? input[index - 1] : 0.0;
            if (tx == TileX - 1)
                tile[ty + 1][TileX + 1] = (x + 1 < nx && y < ny)
                                             ? input[index + 1] : 0.0;
            if (ty == 0)
                tile[0][tx + 1] = load ? input[index - nx] : 0.0;
            if (ty == TileY - 1)
                tile[TileY + 1][tx + 1] = (x < nx && y + 1 < ny)
                                             ? input[index + nx] : 0.0;
            __syncthreads();
            if (update) {
                // Preserve the original addition order and correctly rounded
                // division. Reciprocal multiplication would change hashes.
                const Real sum = center + tile[ty + 1][tx]
                    + tile[ty + 1][tx + 2] + tile[ty][tx + 1]
                    + tile[ty + 2][tx + 1] + below + above;
                output[index] = __ddiv_rn(sum, 7.0);
            }
            __syncthreads();
            below = center;
            center = above;
        }
    }
}

template <bool Boundary>
static void launchStencil(const Real* input, Real* output, size_t nx,
                          size_t ny, size_t zBegin, size_t zEnd,
                          size_t ownedPlanes, cudaStream_t stream) {
    if (zBegin >= zEnd) return;
    const size_t tilesX = (nx - 3) / TileX + 1;
    const size_t tilesY = (ny - 3) / TileY + 1;
    const size_t tilesZ = Boundary ? (ownedPlanes == 1 ? 1 : 2)
                                   : (zEnd - zBegin - 1) / TileZ + 1;
    const size_t tiles = tilesX * tilesY * tilesZ;
    const unsigned blocks = static_cast<unsigned>(std::min<size_t>(tiles, 65535));
    stencilKernel<Boundary><<<blocks, dim3(TileX, TileY), 0, stream>>>(
        input, output, nx, ny, zBegin, zEnd, ownedPlanes, tilesX, tilesY, tiles);
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
}

static size_t slabCount(size_t nz, int ranks, int rank) {
    return nz / ranks + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < nz % ranks);
}

static size_t slabStart(size_t nz, int ranks, int rank) {
    return (nz / ranks) * rank + std::min<size_t>(rank, nz % ranks);
}

// Chunk MPI's int-sized counts without truncating global dimensions or offsets.
static void postHalo(Real* data, size_t count, int peer, int tag, bool send,
                     MPI_Comm comm, std::vector<MPI_Request>& requests) {
    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < count;) {
        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min<size_t>(count - offset, INT_MAX));
        MPI_Request request;
        if (send)
            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Isend(data + offset, chunk, MPI_DOUBLE, peer, tag,
                               comm, &request));
        else
            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Irecv(data + offset, chunk, MPI_DOUBLE, peer, tag,
                               comm, &request));
        requests.push_back(request);
        offset += chunk;
    }
}

static void gatherGrid(const std::vector<Real>& local, size_t nx, size_t ny,
                       size_t nz, int rank, int ranks, MPI_Comm comm) {
    constexpr int resultTag = 3;
    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
    if (rank == 0) {
        std::vector<Real> global(plane * nz);
        std::copy(local.begin(), local.end(), global.begin());
        for (int source = 1; source < ranks; ++source) {
            const size_t start = slabStart(nz, ranks, source) * plane;
            const size_t count = slabCount(nz, ranks, source) * plane;
            for (size_t offset = 0; offset < count;) {
                const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min<size_t>(count - offset, INT_MAX));
                MPI_CHECK(MPI_Recv(global.data() + start + offset, chunk,
                                   MPI_DOUBLE, source, resultTag, comm, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE));
                offset += chunk;
            }
        }
        // Keep global order for the original Kahan sum, samples and byte hash.
        print_results(global, \"Grid\");
    } else {
        for (size_t offset = 0; offset < local.size();) {
            const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min<size_t>(local.size() - offset, INT_MAX));
            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Send(local.data() + offset, chunk, MPI_DOUBLE,
                               0, resultTag, comm));
            offset += chunk;
        }
    }
}

static bool validateResult(const std::vector<Real>& local, int rank, MPI_Comm comm) {
    Real minVal = std::numeric_limits<Real>::infinity();
    Real maxVal = -std::numeric_limits<Real>::infinity();
    int invalid = 0;
    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(min:minVal) reduction(max:maxVal) reduction(|:invalid)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < local.size(); ++i) {
        const Real value = local[i];
        invalid |= "'!std::isfinite(value);
        minVal = std::min(minVal, value);
        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, value);
    }
    Real globalMin, globalMax;
    int globalInvalid;
    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Allreduce(&minVal, &globalMin, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MIN, comm));
    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Allreduce(&maxVal, &globalMax, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, comm));
    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Allreduce(&invalid, &globalInvalid, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MAX, comm));
    if (rank == 0) {
        if (globalInvalid)
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value'"\\n\");
        else {
            printf(\"Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", globalMin, globalMax);
            if (globalMax > 1e6 || globalMin < -1e6)
                printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");
        }
    }
    return "'!globalInvalid && globalMax <= 1e6 && globalMin >= -1e6;
}

static void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]'"\\n\", progName);
    printf(\"Options:\\n\");
    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\\n\");
    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");
    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");
    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");
    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");
    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");
    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");
}

static bool parseSize(const char* text, size_t& value) {
    if (*text < '0' || *text > '9') return false;
    char* end;
    errno = 0;
    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
    if (errno || *end || parsed > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max()) return false;
    value = static_cast<size_t>(parsed);
    return true;
}

static int runBenchmark(int argc, char** argv, int worldRank, int worldSize) {
    size_t nx = 128, ny = 0, nz = 0;
    int iterations = 10;
    bool validate = false, printResults = false;
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        const char* option = argv[i];
        if (("'!strcmp(option, "-x") || !strcmp(option, "-y") ||
             !strcmp(option, "-z") || !strcmp(option, "-i")) && i + 1 < argc) {
            size_t value;
            if (!parseSize(argv[++i], value) ||
                (!strcmp(option, "-i") && value > INT_MAX)) {
                if (worldRank == 0) fprintf(stderr, "Invalid value for %s: %s'"\\n\", option, argv[i]);
                return 1;
            }
            if ("'!strcmp(option, "-x")) nx = value;
            else if (!strcmp(option, "-y")) ny = value;
            else if (!strcmp(option, "-z")) nz = value;
            else iterations = static_cast<int>(value);
        } else if (!strcmp(option, "-v")) validate = true;
        else if (!strcmp(option, "-r")) printResults = true;
        else if (!strcmp(option, "-h")) {
            if (worldRank == 0) printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            if (worldRank == 0) {
                printf("Unknown option: %s'"\\n\", option);
                printUsage(argv[0]);
            }
            return 1;
        }
    }
    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;
    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;
    const size_t maxCells = std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(Real);
    if ("'!nx || !ny || !nz || nx > maxCells / ny || nx * ny > maxCells / nz) {
        if (worldRank == 0) fprintf(stderr, "Grid dimensions must be positive and fit in address space.'"\\n\");
        return 1;
    }

    // Excess ranks do not allocate a slab or a GPU. Each active rank owns at
    // least one plane, including thin and uneven decompositions.
    const int ranks = static_cast<int>(std::min<size_t>(worldSize, nz));
    MPI_Comm comm;
    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_split(MPI_COMM_WORLD, worldRank < ranks ? 0 : MPI_UNDEFINED,
                             worldRank, &comm));
    if (comm == MPI_COMM_NULL) return 0;
    const int rank = worldRank;
    const size_t owned = slabCount(nz, ranks, rank);
    const size_t startZ = slabStart(nz, ranks, rank);
    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
    if (owned > maxCells / plane || maxCells / plane - owned < 2) {
        fprintf(stderr, \"Rank %d: local grid with halo planes is too large.\\n\", rank);
        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
        return 1;
    }
    const size_t cells = (owned + 2) * plane;
    const size_t bytes = cells * sizeof(Real);
    const size_t planeBytes = plane * sizeof(Real);

    MPI_Comm nodeComm;
    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_split_type(comm, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, rank, MPI_INFO_NULL, &nodeComm));
    int localRank;
    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_rank(nodeComm, &localRank));
    int devices;
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&devices));
    if (devices == 0) {
        fprintf(stderr, \"Rank %d: a CUDA GPU is required.\\n\", rank);
        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
        return 1;
    }
    // Supports launchers that expose only the assigned GPU to each rank.
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(localRank % devices));
    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_free(&nodeComm));
    if ("'!std::getenv("OMP_NUM_THREADS"))
        omp_set_num_threads(std::min(8, omp_get_max_threads()));

    int cudaAware = 0;
#ifdef STENCIL3D_MPI_CUDA_QUERY
    cudaAware = MPIX_Query_cuda_support() != 0;
#endif
    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Allreduce(MPI_IN_PLACE, &cudaAware, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MIN, comm));
    if (rank == 0) {
        printf("3D Stencil Benchmark'"\\n\");
        printf(\"Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\\n\", nx, ny, nz);
        printf(\"Iterations: %d\\n\", iterations);
        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");
        printf(\"Parallelism: %d MPI ranks, %d OpenMP threads/rank, CUDA (%s halos)\\n\",
               ranks, omp_get_max_threads(), cudaAware ? \"device\" : \"pinned host\");
        printf(\"Initializing grid...\\n\");
    }

    Real *input, *output;
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&input), bytes));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&output), bytes));
    {
        std::vector<Real> initial(cells);
        #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
        for (size_t i = 0; i < cells; ++i) {
            const bool outside = (startZ == 0 && i < plane) ||
                (startZ + owned == nz && i >= (owned + 1) * plane);
            initial[i] = outside ? 0.0 : static_cast<Real>((startZ * plane + i - plane) % 19);
        }
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(input, initial.data(), bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
        // Seed fixed faces in both buffers to avoid per-iteration copies.
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(output, initial.data(), bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
    }

    cudaStream_t bulkStream, edgeStream, transfer[2];
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&bulkStream, cudaStreamNonBlocking));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&edgeStream, cudaStreamNonBlocking));
    cudaEvent_t bulkDone, edgeDone, sendReady[2], haloReady[2];
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreateWithFlags(&bulkDone, cudaEventDisableTiming));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreateWithFlags(&edgeDone, cudaEventDisableTiming));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(bulkDone, bulkStream));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(edgeDone, edgeStream));
    Real *sendBuffer[2] = {nullptr, nullptr}, *recvBuffer[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};
    const int peers[2] = {rank == 0 ? MPI_PROC_NULL : rank - 1,
                          rank + 1 == ranks ? MPI_PROC_NULL : rank + 1};
    for (int side = 0; side < 2; ++side) {
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&transfer[side], cudaStreamNonBlocking));
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreateWithFlags(&sendReady[side], cudaEventDisableTiming));
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreateWithFlags(&haloReady[side], cudaEventDisableTiming));
        if ("'!cudaAware && peers[side] != MPI_PROC_NULL && iterations > 1 && nx > 2 && ny > 2 && nz > 2) {
            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&sendBuffer[side]), planeBytes));
            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&recvBuffer[side]), planeBytes));
        }
    }
    std::vector<MPI_Request> requests;
    requests.reserve(4 * ((plane - 1) / INT_MAX + 1));
    const size_t zBegin = startZ == 0 ? 2 : 1;
    const size_t zEnd = owned + 1 - (startZ + owned == nz);
    const bool hasInterior = nx > 2 && ny > 2 && nz > 2;

    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Barrier(comm));
    if (rank == 0) printf("Running stencil computation...'"\\n\");
    const double begin = MPI_Wtime();
    for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations && hasInterior; ++iter) {
        if (ranks == 1) {
            launchStencil<false>(input, output, nx, ny, zBegin, zEnd, owned, bulkStream);
        } else {
            // Capture the PREVIOUS events before re-recording either event.
            // The two kernels write disjoint output planes. MPI can send the
            // previous edges while the previous bulk kernel is still running.
            CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamWaitEvent(bulkStream, edgeDone, 0));
            CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamWaitEvent(edgeStream, bulkDone, 0));
            launchStencil<false>(input, output, nx, ny, std::max<size_t>(zBegin, 2),
                                 std::min(zEnd, owned), owned, bulkStream);
            CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(bulkDone, bulkStream));

            // Initial ghost planes have already been initialized analytically.
            if (iter > 0) {
                requests.clear();
                for (int side = 0; side < 2; ++side) {
                    if (peers[side] == MPI_PROC_NULL) continue;
                    Real* ghost = input + (side == 0 ? 0 : owned + 1) * plane;
                    postHalo(cudaAware ? ghost : recvBuffer[side], plane,
                             peers[side], 1 - side, false, comm, requests);
                    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamWaitEvent(transfer[side], edgeDone, 0));
                    if ("'!cudaAware)
                        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(sendBuffer[side],
                            input + (side == 0 ? 1 : owned) * plane, planeBytes,
                            cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, transfer[side]));
                    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(sendReady[side], transfer[side]));
                }
                for (int side = 0; side < 2; ++side) {
                    if (peers[side] == MPI_PROC_NULL) continue;
                    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(sendReady[side]));
                    postHalo(cudaAware ? input + (side == 0 ? 1 : owned) * plane
                                       : sendBuffer[side], plane, peers[side], side,
                             true, comm, requests);
                }
                // Drive MPI progress while the GPU does bulk work.
                for (size_t offset = 0; offset < requests.size();) {
                    const int count = static_cast<int>(std::min<size_t>(requests.size() - offset, INT_MAX));
                    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Waitall(count, requests.data() + offset, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE));
                    offset += count;
                }
                for (int side = 0; side < 2; ++side) {
                    if (peers[side] == MPI_PROC_NULL) continue;
                    if (!cudaAware)
                        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(
                            input + (side == 0 ? 0 : owned + 1) * plane,
                            recvBuffer[side], planeBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice,
                            transfer[side]));
                    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(haloReady[side], transfer[side]));
                    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamWaitEvent(edgeStream, haloReady[side], 0));
                }
            }
            launchStencil<true>(input, output, nx, ny, zBegin, zEnd, owned, edgeStream);
            CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(edgeDone, edgeStream));
        }
        std::swap(input, output);
    }
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(bulkStream));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(edgeStream));
    const double elapsed = MPI_Wtime() - begin;
    double seconds;
    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Reduce(&elapsed, &seconds, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, comm));
    if (rank == 0) {
        const double updates = hasInterior ? static_cast<double>(nx - 2) *
            static_cast<double>(ny - 2) * static_cast<double>(nz - 2) * iterations : 0.0;
        printf("Computation time: %.3f ms'"\\n\", seconds * 1000.0);
        printf(\"Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\\n\", seconds > 0.0 ? updates / seconds / 1e6 : 0.0);
    }

    bool valid = true;
    if (validate || printResults) {
        std::vector<Real> local(owned * plane);
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(local.data(), input + plane, local.size() * sizeof(Real),
                              cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
        if (printResults) gatherGrid(local, nx, ny, nz, rank, ranks, comm);
        if (validate) {
            if (rank == 0) printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");
            valid = validateResult(local, rank, comm);
            if (rank == 0) printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", valid ? \"PASSED\" : \"FAILED\");
        }
    }
    for (int side = 0; side < 2; ++side) {
        if (sendBuffer[side]) CUDA_CHECK(cudaFreeHost(sendBuffer[side]));
        if (recvBuffer[side]) CUDA_CHECK(cudaFreeHost(recvBuffer[side]));
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(sendReady[side]));
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(haloReady[side]));
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamDestroy(transfer[side]));
    }
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(bulkDone));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(edgeDone));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamDestroy(bulkStream));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamDestroy(edgeStream));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(input));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(output));
    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_free(&comm));
    return valid ? 0 : 1;
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    int provided;
    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &provided));
    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_set_errhandler(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_ERRORS_RETURN));
    if (provided < MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED) {
        fprintf(stderr, \"MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED support is required.\\n\");
        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
        return 1;
    }
    int rank, ranks;
    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank));
    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &ranks));
    int result;
    try {
        result = runBenchmark(argc, argv, rank, ranks);
    } catch (const std::exception& error) {
        fprintf(stderr, \"Rank %d: %s\\n\", rank, error.what());
        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
        return 1;
    }
    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Finalize());
    return result;
}

+    "$<$<COMPILE_LANGUAGE:CUDA>:-O3;--fmad=false;-Xcompiler=-O3,-Wall,-Wextra>"
+    "$<$<COMPILE_LANGUAGE:CXX>:-O3;-Wall;-Wextra>")

-target_link_libraries(stencil3d m)
+# Query CUDA-aware MPI at runtime when the MPI implementation supports it.
+# Other installations use asynchronous pinned-host staging of halo planes.
+include(CheckCXXSourceCompiles)
+set(CMAKE_REQUIRED_LIBRARIES MPI::MPI_CXX)
+check_cxx_source_compiles("
+    #include <mpi.h>
+    #if __has_include(<mpi-ext.h>)
+    #include <mpi-ext.h>
+    #endif
+    int main() { return MPIX_Query_cuda_support(); }
+" STENCIL3D_MPI_CUDA_QUERY)
+unset(CMAKE_REQUIRED_LIBRARIES)
+if(STENCIL3D_MPI_CUDA_QUERY)
+    target_compile_definitions(stencil3d PRIVATE STENCIL3D_MPI_CUDA_QUERY)
+endif()
diff --git a/stencil3d/stencil3d.cpp b/stencil3d/stencil3d.cpp
index ace1b3797afe66db79450ce36f30d458d55d9ae4..3f326eb66778bd923a35a59b4c52483e2e1dff53
--- a/stencil3d/stencil3d.cpp
+++ b/stencil3d/stencil3d.cpp
@@ -386,6 +386,10 @@
                 requests.clear();
                 for (int side = 0; side < 2; ++side) {
                     if (peers[side] == MPI_PROC_NULL) continue;
+                    // MPI must not overwrite a pinned receive buffer while
+                    // the preceding asynchronous H2D copy still reads it.
+                    if (!cudaAware && iter > 1)
+                        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(haloReady[side]));
                     Real* ghost = input + (side == 0 ? 0 : owned + 1) * plane;
                     postHalo(cudaAware ? ghost : recvBuffer[side], plane,
                              peers[side], 1 - side, false, comm, requests);

/bin/bash -lc "python3 - <<'PY'
import os, subprocess, re
env = dict(os.environ, OMP_NUM_THREADS='4')
binary = '/tmp/stencil3d-hybrid-build/stencil3d'
cases = [
    (1,1,1,0,1), (1,1,1,5,4),
    (2,5,7,6,3), (7,2,5,5,4), (7,9,2,8,4),
    (3,3,3,1,1), (3,3,3,7,4),
    (17,13,11,0,3), (17,13,11,1,4), (17,13,11,2,4),
    (17,13,11,19,1), (17,13,11,19,2), (17,13,11,19,3),
    (17,13,11,19,4), (17,13,3,9,8), (17,13,4,10,4),
    (34,10,17,8,4), (35,11,17,9,3), (33,9,5,11,4),
    (67,19,37,12,4), (128,128,128,10,1), (128,128,128,10,4),
    (131,67,21,31,4), (65,17,9,100,4)
]
def results(output):
    match = re.search(r'=== RESULTS ===.*?=== END RESULTS ===', output, re.S)
    assert match, output
    return match.group()
for n, (x,y,z,it,ranks) in enumerate(cases, 1):
    args = ['-x',str(x),'-y',str(y),'-z',str(z),'-i',str(it),'-r','-v']
    original = subprocess.run(['/tmp/stencil3d-reference', *args], check=True, text=True, capture_output=True, timeout=30)
    hybrid = subprocess.run(['mpirun','--allow-run-as-root','--oversubscribe','--bind-to','none','-np',str(ranks),binary,*args],
                            env=env, check=True, text=True, capture_output=True, timeout=60)
    assert results(original.stdout) == results(hybrid.stdout), (x,y,z,it,ranks,original.stdout,hybrid.stdout)
    assert hybrid.stdout.count('Validation: PASSED') == 1, hybrid.stdout
    print(f'PASS {n}/{len(cases)}: {x}x{y}x{z}, {it} iterations, {ranks} ranks', flush=True)
print('All result blocks, including byte hashes, match the original.')
PY" in /home/llmtest/evals/20261006-204623/stencil3d_gpt-6.1-sol-xhigh_hybrid_r2

import os, subprocess
binary='/tmp/stencil3d-hybrid-build/stencil3d'
env=dict(os.environ, OMP_NUM_THREADS='2')
launch=['mpirun','--allow-run-as-root','--bind-to','none','-np','2',binary]
for args in (['-h'], ['-x','0'], ['-x','-3'], ['-i','-1'], ['-x','abc'], ['-i','2147483648'], ['-x','18446744073709551615'], ['-x']):
    run=subprocess.run(launch+args, env=env, text=True, capture_output=True, timeout=15)
    assert (run.returncode == 0) == (args == ['-h']), (args, run.returncode, run.stdout, run.stderr)
    if args == ['-h']:
        assert run.stdout.count('Usage:') == 1, run.stdout
    print('PASS CLI:', ' '.join(args))
for args in (['-x','19','-i','3'], ['-x','19','-i','3','-v']):
    run=subprocess.run(launch+args, env=env, text=True, capture_output=True, timeout=20, check=True)
    assert '=== RESULTS ===' not in run.stdout
    if '-v' in args:
        assert 'Validation: PASSED' in run.stdout
    print('PASS output mode:', ' '.join(args))

+++ b/stencil3d/CMakeLists.txt
@@ -1,13 +1,49 @@
-cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.10)
-project(stencil3d_benchmark)
+cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.24)
+
+# Set CMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES explicitly when building for a different or
+# heterogeneous accelerator cluster. Otherwise optimize for the local GPUs.
+if(NOT DEFINED CMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES)
+    set(CMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES native)
+endif()
+project(stencil3d_benchmark LANGUAGES CXX CUDA)
 
 set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD 20)
 set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)
+set(CMAKE_CUDA_STANDARD 17)
+set(CMAKE_CUDA_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)
 
+find_package(MPI REQUIRED COMPONENTS CXX)
+find_package(OpenMP REQUIRED COMPONENTS CXX)
+find_package(CUDAToolkit REQUIRED)
+
+# Compile the existing source as CUDA without renaming it or adding files.
+set_source_files_properties(stencil3d.cpp PROPERTIES LANGUAGE CUDA)
 add_executable(stencil3d stencil3d.cpp)
+target_compile_definitions(stencil3d PRIVATE OMPI_SKIP_MPICXX MPICH_SKIP_MPICXX)
+target_link_libraries(stencil3d PRIVATE MPI::MPI_CXX OpenMP::OpenMP_CXX CUDA::cudart m)
 
-# Enable optimizations
-target_compile_options(stencil3d PRIVATE -O3 -march=native -Wall -Wextra)
+# The imported OpenMP target supplies host libraries; nvcc also needs the host
+# compiler's OpenMP switches forwarded explicitly during compilation.
+separate_arguments(stencil3d_openmp_flags NATIVE_COMMAND "${OpenMP_CXX_FLAGS}")
+foreach(flag IN LISTS stencil3d_openmp_flags)
+    target_compile_options(stencil3d PRIVATE "$<$<COMPILE_LANGUAGE:CUDA>:-Xcompiler=${flag}>")
+endforeach()
+target_compile_options(stencil3d PRIVATE
+    "$<$<COMPILE_LANGUAGE:CUDA>:-O3;--fmad=false;-Xcompiler=-O3,-Wall,-Wextra>"
+    "$<$<COMPILE_LANGUAGE:CXX>:-O3;-Wall;-Wextra>")
 
-# Link math library
-target_link_libraries(stencil3d m)
+# Query CUDA-aware MPI at runtime when the MPI implementation supports it.
+# Other installations use asynchronous pinned-host staging of halo planes.
+include(CheckCXXSourceCompiles)
+set(CMAKE_REQUIRED_LIBRARIES MPI::MPI_CXX)
+check_cxx_source_compiles("
+    #include <mpi.h>
+    #if __has_include(<mpi-ext.h>)
+    #include <mpi-ext.h>
+    #endif
+    int main() { return MPIX_Query_cuda_support(); }
+" STENCIL3D_MPI_CUDA_QUERY)
+unset(CMAKE_REQUIRED_LIBRARIES)
+if(STENCIL3D_MPI_CUDA_QUERY)
+    target_compile_definitions(stencil3d PRIVATE STENCIL3D_MPI_CUDA_QUERY)
+endif()
diff --git a/stencil3d/stencil3d.cpp b/stencil3d/stencil3d.cpp
index ace1b3797afe66db79450ce36f30d458d55d9ae4..3f326eb66778bd923a35a59b4c52483e2e1dff53

@@ -386,6 +386,10 @@
                 requests.clear();
                 for (int side = 0; side < 2; ++side) {
                     if (peers[side] == MPI_PROC_NULL) continue;
+                    // MPI must not overwrite a pinned receive buffer while
+                    // the preceding asynchronous H2D copy still reads it.
+                    if (!cudaAware && iter > 1)
+                        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(haloReady[side]));
                     Real* ghost = input + (side == 0 ? 0 : owned + 1) * plane;
                     postHalo(cudaAware ? ghost : recvBuffer[side], plane,
                              peers[side], 1 - side, false, comm, requests);

/bin/bash -lc "python3 - <<'PY'
import os, subprocess, re
env=dict(os.environ, OMP_NUM_THREADS='4')
for n,iterations in [(256,40),(512,30)]:
    for ranks in (1,2,4):
        command=['mpirun','--allow-run-as-root','--bind-to','none','-np',str(ranks),
                 '/tmp/stencil3d-hybrid-build/stencil3d','-x',str(n),'-i',str(iterations)]
        run=subprocess.run(command, env=env, check=True, text=True, capture_output=True, timeout=90)
        timing=re.search(r'Computation time:.*',run.stdout).group()
        perf=re.search(r'Performance:.*',run.stdout).group()
        print(f'{n}"'^3 x {iterations}, {ranks} GPUs: {timing}; {perf}'"', flush=True)
run=subprocess.run(['/tmp/stencil3d-reference','-x','256','-i','40'],check=True,text=True,capture_output=True,timeout=90)
print('Original 256"'^3 x 40: '"'+re.search(r'Computation time:.*',run.stdout).group(),flush=True)
PY" in /home/llmtest/evals/20261006-204623/stencil3d_gpt-6.1-sol-xhigh_hybrid_r2

+# Other installations use asynchronous pinned-host staging of halo planes.
+include(CheckCXXSourceCompiles)
+set(CMAKE_REQUIRED_LIBRARIES MPI::MPI_CXX)
+check_cxx_source_compiles("
+    #include <mpi.h>
+    #if __has_include(<mpi-ext.h>)
+    #include <mpi-ext.h>
+    #endif
+    int main() { return MPIX_Query_cuda_support(); }
+" STENCIL3D_MPI_CUDA_QUERY)

+endif()

@@ -386,6 +386,10 @@
                 requests.clear();
                 for (int side = 0; side < 2; ++side) {
                     if (peers[side] == MPI_PROC_NULL) continue;
+                    // MPI must not overwrite a pinned receive buffer while
+                    // the preceding asynchronous H2D copy still reads it.
+                    if (!cudaAware && iter > 1)
+                        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(haloReady[side]));
                     Real* ghost = input + (side == 0 ? 0 : owned + 1) * plane;
                     postHalo(cudaAware ? ghost : recvBuffer[side], plane,
                              peers[side], 1 - side, false, comm, requests);

#define MPI_CHECK(call) mpiCheck((call), #call)
#define CUDA_CHECK(call) cudaCheck((call), #call)

constexpr unsigned TileX = 32;
constexpr unsigned TileY = 8;
constexpr size_t TileZ = 8;

// Coalesced XY tiles share their four lateral neighbors. A register window
// reuses the center and the two Z neighbors while sweeping eight planes.
// Boundary mode updates just the two MPI faces, independently of bulk work.
template <bool Boundary>
__global__ void stencilKernel(const Real* __restrict__ input,
                              Real* __restrict__ output,
                              size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t zBegin,
                              size_t zEnd, size_t ownedPlanes,
                              size_t tilesX, size_t tilesY, size_t tilesZ) {
    __shared__ Real tile[TileY + 2][TileX + 2];
    const unsigned tx = threadIdx.x;
    const unsigned ty = threadIdx.y;
    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
    // A native 3D launch avoids costly 64-bit division to decode tile indices.
    // Grid-stride loops still support dimensions beyond CUDA's grid limits.
    for (size_t bz = blockIdx.z; bz < tilesZ; bz += gridDim.z) {
      for (size_t by = blockIdx.y; by < tilesY; by += gridDim.y) {
       for (size_t bx = blockIdx.x; bx < tilesX; bx += gridDim.x) {
        const size_t x = bx * TileX + tx + 1;
        const size_t y = by * TileY + ty + 1;
        const size_t first = Boundary ? (bz == 0 ? 1 : ownedPlanes)
                                     : zBegin + bz * TileZ;
        const size_t last = Boundary ? first + 1
                                    : (first + TileZ < zEnd ? first + TileZ : zEnd);
        // This predicate is uniform across the block.
        if (first < zBegin || first >= zEnd) continue;
        const bool load = x < nx && y < ny;
        const bool update = x < nx - 1 && y < ny - 1;
        size_t index = first * plane + y * nx + x;
        Real below = load ? input[index - plane] : 0.0;
        Real center = load ? input[index] : 0.0;
        for (size_t z = first; z < last; ++z, index += plane) {
            const Real above = load ? input[index + plane] : 0.0;
            tile[ty + 1][tx + 1] = center;
            if (tx == 0)
                tile[ty + 1][0] = load ? input[index - 1] : 0.0;
            if (tx == TileX - 1)
                tile[ty + 1][TileX + 1] = (x + 1 < nx && y < ny)
                                             ? input[index + 1] : 0.0;
            if (ty == 0)
                tile[0][tx + 1] = load ? input[index - nx] : 0.0;
            if (ty == TileY - 1)
                tile[TileY + 1][tx + 1] = (x < nx && y + 1 < ny)
                                             ? input[index + nx] : 0.0;
            __syncthreads();
            if (update) {
                // Preserve the original addition order and correctly rounded
                // division. Reciprocal multiplication would change hashes.
                const Real sum = center + tile[ty + 1][tx]
                    + tile[ty + 1][tx + 2] + tile[ty][tx + 1]
                    + tile[ty + 2][tx + 1] + below + above;
                output[index] = __ddiv_rn(sum, 7.0);
            }
            __syncthreads();
            below = center;
            center = above;
        }
       }
      }
    }
}

template <bool Boundary>
static void launchStencil(const Real* input, Real* output, size_t nx,
                          size_t ny, size_t zBegin, size_t zEnd,
                          size_t ownedPlanes, cudaStream_t stream) {
    if (zBegin >= zEnd) return;
    const size_t tilesX = (nx - 3) / TileX + 1;
    const size_t tilesY = (ny - 3) / TileY + 1;
    const size_t tilesZ = Boundary ? (ownedPlanes == 1 ? 1 : 2)
                                   : (zEnd - zBegin - 1) / TileZ + 1;
    const dim3 blocks(static_cast<unsigned>(std::min<size_t>(tilesX, INT_MAX)),
                      static_cast<unsigned>(std::min<size_t>(tilesY, 65535)),
                      static_cast<unsigned>(std::min<size_t>(tilesZ, 65535)));
    stencilKernel<Boundary><<<blocks, dim3(TileX, TileY), 0, stream>>>(
        input, output, nx, ny, zBegin, zEnd, ownedPlanes, tilesX, tilesY, tilesZ);
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
}

static size_t slabCount(size_t nz, int ranks, int rank) {
    return nz / ranks + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < nz % ranks);
}

static size_t slabStart(size_t nz, int ranks, int rank) {
    return (nz / ranks) * rank + std::min<size_t>(rank, nz % ranks);
}

// Chunk MPI's int-sized counts without truncating global dimensions or offsets.
static void postHalo(Real* data, size_t count, int peer, int tag, bool send,

+                    // the preceding asynchronous H2D copy still reads it.
+                    if (!cudaAware && iter > 1)
+                        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(haloReady[side]));
                     Real* ghost = input + (side == 0 ? 0 : owned + 1) * plane;
                     postHalo(cudaAware ? ghost : recvBuffer[side], plane,
                              peers[side], 1 - side, false, comm, requests);

from pathlib import Path
p=Path('stencil3d/stencil3d.cpp')
s=p.read_text()
start=s.index('    for (size_t bz = blockIdx.z;')
end=s.index('\\n}\\n\\ntemplate <bool Boundary>\\nstatic void launchStencil',start)
part=s[start:end]
lines=part.splitlines()
lines[1]='        for (size_t by = blockIdx.y; by < tilesY; by += gridDim.y) {'
lines[2]='            for (size_t bx = blockIdx.x; bx < tilesX; bx += gridDim.x) {'
for i in range(3,len(lines)-3):
    lines[i]='        '+lines[i]
lines[-3]='            }'
lines[-2]='        }'
s=s[:start]+'\\n'.join(lines)+s[end:]
p.write_text(s)
PY

// reuses the center and the two Z neighbors while sweeping eight planes.
// Boundary mode updates just the two MPI faces, independently of bulk work.
template <bool Boundary>
__global__ void stencilKernel(const Real* __restrict__ input,
                              Real* __restrict__ output,
                              size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t zBegin,
                              size_t zEnd, size_t ownedPlanes,
                              size_t tilesX, size_t tilesY, size_t tilesZ) {
    __shared__ Real tile[2][TileY + 2][TileX + 2];
    const unsigned tx = threadIdx.x;
    const unsigned ty = threadIdx.y;
    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
    // A native 3D launch avoids costly 64-bit division to decode tile indices.
    // Grid-stride loops still support dimensions beyond CUDA's grid limits.
    for (size_t bz = blockIdx.z; bz < tilesZ; bz += gridDim.z) {
        for (size_t by = blockIdx.y; by < tilesY; by += gridDim.y) {
            for (size_t bx = blockIdx.x; bx < tilesX; bx += gridDim.x) {
                const size_t x = bx * TileX + tx + 1;
                const size_t y = by * TileY + ty + 1;
                const size_t first = Boundary ? (bz == 0 ? 1 : ownedPlanes)
                                             : zBegin + bz * TileZ;
                const size_t last = Boundary ? first + 1
                                            : (first + TileZ < zEnd ? first + TileZ : zEnd);
                // This predicate is uniform across the block.
                if (first < zBegin || first >= zEnd) continue;
                const bool load = x < nx && y < ny;
                const bool update = x < nx - 1 && y < ny - 1;
                size_t index = first * plane + y * nx + x;
                Real below = load ? input[index - plane] : 0.0;
                Real center = load ? input[index] : 0.0;
                for (size_t z = first; z < last; ++z, index += plane) {
                    auto& layer = tile[(z - first) & 1];
                    const Real above = load ? input[index + plane] : 0.0;
                    layer[ty + 1][tx + 1] = center;
                    if (tx == 0)
                        layer[ty + 1][0] = load ? input[index - 1] : 0.0;
                    if (tx == TileX - 1)
                        layer[ty + 1][TileX + 1] = (x + 1 < nx && y < ny)
                                                     ? input[index + 1] : 0.0;
                    if (ty == 0)
                        layer[0][tx + 1] = load ? input[index - nx] : 0.0;
                    if (ty == TileY - 1)
                        layer[TileY + 1][tx + 1] = (x < nx && y + 1 < ny)
                                                     ? input[index + nx] : 0.0;
                    __syncthreads();
                    if (update) {
                        // Preserve the original addition order and correctly rounded
                        // division. Reciprocal multiplication would change hashes.
                        const Real sum = center + layer[ty + 1][tx]
                            + layer[ty + 1][tx + 2] + layer[ty][tx + 1]
                            + layer[ty + 2][tx + 1] + below + above;
                        output[index] = __ddiv_rn(sum, 7.0);
                    }
                    // Alternate shared layers: the next iteration's barrier protects
                    // the preceding reads, avoiding a second barrier on every plane.
                    below = center;
                    center = above;
                }
                __syncthreads();
            }
        }
    }
}

template <bool Boundary>
static void launchStencil(const Real* input, Real* output, size_t nx,
                          size_t ny, size_t zBegin, size_t zEnd,
                          size_t ownedPlanes, cudaStream_t stream) {
    if (zBegin >= zEnd) return;
    const size_t tilesX = (nx - 3) / TileX + 1;
    const size_t tilesY = (ny - 3) / TileY + 1;
    const size_t tilesZ = Boundary ? (ownedPlanes == 1 ? 1 : 2)
                                   : (zEnd - zBegin - 1) / TileZ + 1;
    const dim3 blocks(static_cast<unsigned>(std::min<size_t>(tilesX, INT_MAX)),
                      static_cast<unsigned>(std::min<size_t>(tilesY, 65535)),
                      static_cast<unsigned>(std::min<size_t>(tilesZ, 65535)));
    stencilKernel<Boundary><<<blocks, dim3(TileX, TileY), 0, stream>>>(
        input, output, nx, ny, zBegin, zEnd, ownedPlanes, tilesX, tilesY, tilesZ);
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
}

static size_t slabCount(size_t nz, int ranks, int rank) {
    return nz / ranks + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < nz % ranks);
}

static size_t slabStart(size_t nz, int ranks, int rank) {
    return (nz / ranks) * rank + std::min<size_t>(rank, nz % ranks);
}

// Chunk MPI's int-sized counts without truncating global dimensions or offsets.
static void postHalo(Real* data, size_t count, int peer, int tag, bool send,

def result(text):
    match=re.search(r'=== RESULTS ===.*?=== END RESULTS ===',text,re.S)
    assert match, text
    return match.group()
for n,(x,y,z,it,ranks) in enumerate(cases,1):
    args=['-x',str(x),'-y',str(y),'-z',str(z),'-i',str(it),'-r','-v']
    env=dict(os.environ, OMP_NUM_THREADS=str((1,3,4)[n%3]))
    original=subprocess.run(['/tmp/stencil3d-reference',*args],check=True,text=True,capture_output=True,timeout=30)
    hybrid=subprocess.run(['mpirun','--allow-run-as-root','--oversubscribe','--bind-to','none','-np',str(ranks),binary,*args],
                          env=env,check=True,text=True,capture_output=True,timeout=60)
    assert result(original.stdout)==result(hybrid.stdout),(x,y,z,it,ranks,original.stdout,hybrid.stdout)
    assert hybrid.stdout.count('Validation: PASSED')==1,hybrid.stdout
    print(f'PASS {n}/{len(cases)}: {x}x{y}x{z}, {it} iterations, {ranks} ranks, {env[\"OMP_NUM_THREADS\"]} threads',flush=True)
print('All 26 final result blocks match the original, including exact byte hashes.',flush=True)

codex
Updated the two existing files; executable remains `stencil3d`.

- Requires MPI, OpenMP, and CUDA.
- Uses GPU-resident tiled stencils with overlapped halo exchange.
- All 26 correctness cases match the original exactly, including hashes.
- CUDA memory, race, and synchronization checks pass.
- Four GPUs achieved approximately 3× single-GPU throughput on `512³`.

```bash
cmake -S stencil3d -B /tmp/stencil3d-build
cmake --build /tmp/stencil3d-build -j
OMP_NUM_THREADS=4 mpirun --bind-to none -np 4 /tmp/stencil3d-build/stencil3d -v
```
